On the training side, we further design Visual Context Segmentation, a sliding window over recent visual context that balances rollout and training-engine pressure, yielding a 2.83x training speedup over step-wise decomposition. Together, ScaleCUA achieves 68.7% on OSWorld and 54.0% on ScienceBoard, establishing new state-of-the-art performance among open-source computer use agents. Code, models, and datasets are available at https://github.com/THUDM/SCALE-CUA.

</details>


### [183] [What We Talk About When We Talk About LLM Planning: Evidence for Two Distinct Planning Abilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11197)
*Sukai Huang,Chenyuan Zhang,Fucai Ke,Zhixi Cai,Naim Rastgoo,Gholamreza Haffari,Hamid Rezatofighi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs在规划任务中的性能差异并非仅由任务难度决定，而是反映了两种不同的潜在规划能力：操作推理（评估行动适用性与状态转换）和结构枚举（推理目标可达性与地标结构）。这两种能力对模型规模、推理时间的响应不同，表明应从能力层面而非整体层面评估LLM规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将LLM在规划任务上的性能不均归因于任务难度，但这一解释不完整，因为任务层面的差异可能反映不同的潜在规划能力，而非单一能力谱系上的差异。作者希望通过多维项目反应理论模型揭示LLM规划背后的潜在能力结构。

Method: 在ACPBench-Hard基准上评估多个LLM家族在不同测试时推理预算下的表现，并应用多维项目反应理论（MIRT）模型分析LLM规划的潜在能力结构。

Result: 发现了两个影响规划性能的主要维度：操作推理（评估局部行动适用性与即时状态转换）和结构枚举（推理目标可达性与地标结构）。模型扩展和更长推理链可提升操作推理能力，但结构枚举对此相对不敏感。

Conclusion: 应转向能力层面的LLM规划评估，关注哪些规划能力得到提升、在什么条件下提升以及为何提升，而非仅仅关注模型整体性能是否提高。

Abstract: When LLMs exhibit uneven performance across planning tasks, these gaps are often attributed to task difficulty. We argue that this explanation is incomplete, as task-level variation may reflect distinct latent planning competencies rather than differences along a single ability spectrum. We study this question on ACPBench-Hard by evaluating multiple LLM families under varying test-time reasoning budgets and applying a multidimensional item response theory model to uncover the latent competency structure underlying LLM planning. The analysis reveals two principal dimensions that shape planning performance: operational reasoning, the ability to evaluate local action applicability and immediate state transitions, and structural enumeration, the ability to reason about goal reachability and landmark structure. Operational reasoning improving under model scaling and longer reasoning traces, while structural enumeration remains comparatively insensitive. Our findings motivate competency-level evaluation of LLM planning, shifting the focus from whether models improve overall to which planning competencies improve, under what conditions, and why.

</details>


### [184] [PREF-Gate: Provenance-Constrained Relational Evidence Fusion with Validation-Gated Selection for Graph Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11212)
*Liming Liu,Chao Hu,Mingfei Lu,Yiwei Ge,Xingle Li,Heyuan Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PREF-Gate框架，通过可审计的门控机制区分无标签图上下文和有标签邻域证据，仅在验证集支持时才使用标签衍生证据，在三个欺诈检测数据集上取得 competitive 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系型欺诈检测中，无标签图上下文和标签衍生邻域证据遵循不同的有效性条件，尤其是邻域风险在查询节点的自身标签或验证/测试标签泄漏时会失效。现有方法缺乏对这种标签来源约束的明确处理。

Method: 提出PREF-Gate，包含两个固定专家（上下文专家仅用无标签特征；证据专家加入自排除的仅训练标签邻域风险和Empirical-Bayes统计量）和一个有限验证门控机制。门控从两个专家和三个预定义概率混合中选择，并固定决策阈值。

Result: 在Amazon、YelpChi和TFinance上，使用5个分层划分和14个同协议方法，PREF-Gate的平均AUPRC分别为0.9085、0.8104和0.8913。门控在Amazon和YelpChi上全选无标签专家，在TFinance上选证据混合方案。

Conclusion: 标签衍生关系证据并非普遍有效，仅当验证集支持时才可用。框架提供了可审计的决策流水线，包含显式标签来源契约、有限选择策略、失败归因和预算评估。

Abstract: Relational fraud detection can exploit both label-free graph context and label-derived neighborhood evidence, but these two information sources obey different validity conditions. In particular, neighborhood risk becomes invalid when a queried node's own label, or any validation or test label, enters its construction. We formulate this issue as provenance-constrained relational evidence use and present PREF-Gate, an auditable decision framework with two fixed experts and a finite validation gate. The context expert uses attributes, one-hop means, feature residuals, and degree descriptors without labels. The evidence expert adds self-excluded, training-label-only neighborhood risk and empirical-Bayes summaries that expose support, uncertainty, availability, and shrinkage. Before test inference, the gate selects either expert or one of three pre-specified probability mixtures and fixes the decision threshold. On Amazon, YelpChi, and TFinance, using five identical stratified splits and 14 same-protocol methods, PREF-Gate obtains mean AUPRC values of 0.9085, 0.8104, and 0.8913. It selects the label-free expert on all Amazon and YelpChi splits and an evidence mixture on all TFinance splits. Thus, the main result is conditional rather than universal: label-derived relational evidence is useful only where held-out validation supports it. The framework couples competitive ranking performance with an explicit label-provenance contract, finite selection policy, failure accounting, and review-budget evaluation, providing an auditable knowledge-based decision pipeline for graph fraud detection.

</details>


### [185] [Heterogeneous Agent Cohorts for Safe Open-Ended Exploration with Runtime Constraint Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11226)
*Tengjiao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种多角色分工的LLM智能体框架，通过Disrupter（创新）、Validator（安全）、Broker（类比）分工协作，并利用MCTS将失败编译为可继承的约束补丁（Scars），在保证安全性的同时提升探索效率和降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在安全约束与创造能力之间存在两难：静态安全指令限制了探索能力，而自由使用工具和多智能体辩论又导致安全违规频发。作者希望在不牺牲安全的前提下释放智能体的创造性。

Method: 1) 角色分离：Disrupter负责生成非常规方案，Validator在工具网关执行运行时安全检查，Broker引入远距离相关类比；2) 失败编译：通过MCTS将失败案例编码为紧凑的带签名约束补丁（Scars），本地缓存并被子代继承；3) 通信分配分数（CAS）：基于信用的带宽限制机制，在资源约束下降低通信成本。

Result: 在空间语义沙箱实验中（N=20, p<0.01）：1) 智能体群体能到达辩论方法失败的远端目标；2) Validator阻止了所有已执行的违规行为；3) Scars通过避免冗余验证，减少15.1%的token消耗；4) CAS在资源约束下将总token成本降低55.9%。

Conclusion: 通过角色分离和失败经验的结构化复用（Scars），可以在不牺牲安全性的前提下显著提升LLM智能体的创造性探索能力和计算效率，为解决安全与创造力之间的权衡提供了新范式。

Abstract: LLM agents today are caught in an awkward bind. Lock them down with static safety instructions and they rarely venture beyond the obvious; give them free reign with tools and multi-agent debate, and safety violations quickly follow. Rather than forcing a single model to juggle both creativity and caution, we separate the concerns across specialized roles. A Disrupter generates unconventional proposals, a Validator enforces hard runtime checks at the tool gateway, and a Broker pulls in distant but relevant analogies. Failures are not discarded -- they are compiled, via MCTS, into compact, signed constraint patches we call Scars. These patches are cached locally and inherited by future cohorts, turning repeated failures into reusable, low-cost runtime constraints. In a spatial-semantic sandbox (N=20 runs, p<0.01), our cohort reaches remote targets where debate fails, the Validator prevents all executed breaches, and Scars reduce token consumption by 15.1% by avoiding redundant validator checks. Furthermore, credit-based Communication Allocation Scores (CAS) restrict outbound bandwidth, reducing overall token costs by 55.9% under resource constraints.

</details>


### [186] [Bringing Back Rule Induction to Fluid Intelligence Research? An Initial Validation of the ARC-AGI Benchmark in Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11263)
*Jasmin Thelen,Oliver Wilhelm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究首次在人类样本中检验了ARC-AGI基准的心理测量学特性。结果表明，ARC-AGI项目具有良好的心理测量学属性，与图形流体智力存在显著相关（ρ=0.63），但与图形原创性关联较弱，初步支持其作为人类流体智力测量工具的效度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体智力测量存在两种竞争观点：工作记忆容量主导说与关系归纳能力主导说。当前测量实践偏向前者（使用有限的重复规则），而后者虽常见于定义却罕见于实际测量。ARC-AGI基准主要要求规则归纳，被提出作为人类和人工智能系统的流体智力测量工具，但其心理测量学特性尚未在人类样本中检验。

Method: 对100名参与者进行首次研究，使用ARC-AGI项目汇编，通过图形推理测试测量图形流体智力，分析其心理测量学特征及 nomological network（法则网络）。

Result: ARC-AGI项目汇编显示出良好的心理测量学属性；与图形流体智力显著相关（ρ=0.63）；与图形原创性关联较弱。

Conclusion: 初步支持ARC-AGI作为人类流体智力测量工具的效度。建议未来研究纳入更多规则归纳任务及多变量协变量，并系统地将AI基准嵌入人类认知能力的法则网络中以促进跨学科合作。

Abstract: Two competing perspectives on fluid intelligence (gf) measures propose that performance is primarily constrained either by working memory capacity or by the ability to induce novel relations. The first perspective is currently dominant in measurement, as evident from the use of a limited set of recurring rules, whereas the second perspective is reflected in many definitions but rarely present in measurement. The ARC-AGI benchmark predominantly requires rule induction and was proposed as a measure of gf for both humans and artificial systems. However, its psychometric properties have not yet been examined in human samples. We therefore investigated the psychometric characteristics and nomological network of ARC-AGI in a first study with 100 participants. A compilation of ARC-AGI items showed good psychometric properties and correlated substantially with figural fluid intelligence as measured by a figural reasoning test (\r{ho} = .63). Associations with figural originality were weak. These findings provide initial support for the validity of ARC-AGI as a measure of human fluid intelligence. Future research should include more rule induction tasks as well as additional multivariate covariates. This study is unusual by studying a task in humans that was initially designed for machines. We suggest systematically embedding AI benchmarks into the nomological network of human cognitive abilities to enable more systematic evaluation and interdisciplinary cooperation.

</details>


### [187] [Valid $\ne$ Necessary: Diagnosing Latent Inefficiency in Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11266)
*Daeyeop Lee,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现现有推理步骤评估器无法惩罚冗长但有效的推理步骤（过度推理），提出基于信息论的训练无关度量CAID，并应用在PACE后向压缩策略中，在GSM8K等数据集上实现31-53%的token压缩而保持准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT推理虽强大但常因过度推理导致高昂计算成本，现有评估器能检测逻辑错误和事实错误，却无法识别低效但有效的推理步骤，存在关键盲区。

Method: 构建RIV-GSM8K诊断基准集（注入五种低效类型）；提出基于信息论的训练无关度量CAID衡量步骤信息密度；将其应用于PACE后向压缩策略进行步骤剪枝。

Result: 现有评估器在RIV-GSM8K上难以区分低效与必要推理；PACE在GSM8K、StrategyQA、ARC-Challenge上实现31-53%的token压缩且准确率保持不变，优于随机剪枝和PRM基线。

Conclusion: CAID能有效从推理链中蒸馏出信息冗余而不损害推理有效性，验证了信息密度作为评估步骤效率的可行性。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting has significantly advanced the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), yet it often incurs substantial computational costs due to over-reasoning: the generation of redundant, verbose, or irrelevant steps. While existing reasoning step evaluators effectively detect logical fallacies and factual errors, our analysis reveals a critical blind spot: they fail to penalize valid but inefficient reasoning steps that inflate token usage without contributing to the solution. To systematically diagnose this limitation, we introduce RIV-GSM8K, a diagnostic benchmark injected with five distinct types of inefficiencies, including circular reasoning and excessive decomposition. Diagnostic experiments reveal that state-of-the-art evaluators struggle to distinguish these inefficiencies from necessary reasoning. To address this gap, we propose CAID (Context-Aware Information Density), a training-free metric grounded in information theory that identifies low-utility steps. To validate the metric's practical utility, we apply it within PACE, a post-hoc compression strategy. Additional control experiments show that the gains of PACE are not explained by trivial pruning: compared with random step removal and PRM-based compression baselines, it preserves accuracy at substantially higher compression rates. Empirical results on GSM8K, StrategyQA, and ARC-Challenge demonstrate that PACE reduces token consumption by 31-53% while maintaining accuracy, confirming that CAID successfully distills informational froth from reasoning chains without compromising deductive validity.

</details>


### [188] [Efficient Test-Time Optimization for Multi-Agent Proof Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11307)
*Tian-Shuo Liu,Shiyuan Zhang,Zijie Geng,Haoyu Liu,Runjie Xu,Pengyuan Wang,Lei Yuan,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ToMap是一个多智能体框架，将证明自动形式化建模为Decomposer-Formalizer-Prover流水线，通过瓶颈分析将测试时优化集中于分解器（Decomposer）的迭代改进，在ProofFlowBench上相比最佳基线提升19.0%且成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明级自动形式化是长程挑战，需要协调多步证明中的声明、上下文和依赖关系。现有方法要么依赖昂贵的模型训练，要么在推理时进行过度且无引导的修复，缺乏高效且有针对性的优化策略。

Method: 提出ToMap多智能体框架，包含Decomposer-Formalizer-Prover三阶段流水线。通过瓶颈分析识别Decomposer为关键瓶颈，将Formalizer和Prover视为下游执行器，聚焦测试时计算于Decomposer的迭代优化。该优化受GEPA启发，通过形式化验证进展和语义证明准则定义Pareto前沿来引导分解演化。

Result: 在ProofFlowBench上，ToMap在语法正确性和语义忠实性两方面均比之前最佳方法提升19.0%，同时测试时成本更低。缩放分析表明，大部分增益在分解演化的几次迭代内即可获得。

Conclusion: 通过瓶颈导向的测试时优化策略，ToMap高效地提升了证明自动形式化的质量，验证了将计算资源集中于分解阶段而非均匀分配的有效性。

Abstract: Full-proof autoformalization bridges extensive mathematical proofs in natural language with formally validated reasoning, offering a pathway to elevate the ceiling of verifiable mathematical reasoning. Unlike statement-level formalization, proof autoformalization is a long-horizon challenge requiring coordination of claims, contexts, and dependencies across many proof steps, yet has only recently come under focused study. Current approaches either rely on costly model training or apply excessive, unguided repair at inference time. To this end, we introduce ToMap, a multi-agent framework that structures proof autoformalization as a Decomposer-Formalizer-Prover pipeline with efficient test-time optimization guided by formal verification and semantic rubrics for proof quality. Rather than distributing test-time compute across all agents, we perform bottleneck analysis and identify the Decomposer as the critical bottleneck: the quality of its atomic, self-contained proof units directly determines whether downstream agents can successfully formalize and prove each step. ToMap therefore treats the Formalizer and Prover as downstream executors and efficiently focuses test-time compute on Decomposer refinement. This refinement follows a loop inspired by GEPA, evolving prompts over candidate decompositions and using formal verification progress together with semantic proof rubrics to define a Pareto frontier that guides the next decomposition update. Experiments on ProofFlowBench show that ToMap improves over the best previous method by 19.0% when evaluated by both syntactic correctness and semantic faithfulness, while requiring lower test-time cost. Scaling analysis shows that most gains emerge within a few iterations of decomposition evolution, guiding test-time budget selection.

</details>


### [189] [Calibrated e-CUSUM Decoding for Quantized Reasoning Models: Why Token Log-Probability Is the Wrong Observable for Decoding Monitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11317)
*El Hassane Ettifouri,Ayoub Belfatmi,Mahaman Sanoussi Yahaya Alassan,Walid Dahhane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于训练-free的解码控制器，用于监控低比特量化小模型在链式推理中的退化问题。该控制器结合了退化感知警报分数（融合token不确定性和显式逐字重复）和校准的类e-process序贯检测器，替代了传统的中心化token对数概率增量（被证明不适合作为监测指标）。实验表明，该方法在GSM8K任务上能够将误报率从93-95%降至有选择性的检测器（φ≈0.3，精确率≈0.6），并使INT4精度从63%提升至69%（p=0.18，统计不显著），代价是28%的token预算。主要贡献是方法论层面的：解释了中心化token对数概率为何不适合解码器监控，并提供了校准且谨慎评估的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化使小型推理模型部署成本降低，但会损害其思维链（CoT）质量。这促使研究者开发解码器端的监控机制，在生成变得不可靠时进行干预。然而，自然候选指标——中心化token对数概率增量被证明在数学上不适合此目的，需要更有效的监测方案。

Method: 提出一种训练-free的解码控制器，包含两部分：(1) 退化感知警报分数，融合token不确定性和显式逐字重复；(2) 校准的类e-process序贯检测器。原始乘积过程在条件均值零假设下满足Ville有效性，而部署的CUSUM下限统计量作为经验变化检测器使用（因分数具有历史依赖性和自相关性）。

Result: 在GSM8K上使用DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B（FP16和INT4）进行实验：校准将原先93-95%误报率的监控器转变为有选择性的检测器（φ≈0.3，精确率≈0.6，基线0.38）。INT4准确率从63%提升至69%（配对McNemar检验p=0.18，n=100，统计不显著），代价是28%的token预算。还发现非终止（而非循环）是GSM8K上的主要失败模式。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是方法论层面：解释了中心化token对数概率增量为何不适合解码器监控（它测量的是采样自一致性而非轨迹健康度，在自信重复时几乎不发出信号），并提供了一个校准且谨慎评估的替代方案。该方案虽然在统计上尚不显著，但展示了减少退化信号和潜在提升量化模型准确率的可能性。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization makes small reasoning models inexpensive to deploy but can degrade their chains of thought. This motivates decoder-side monitors that intervene when generation becomes unreliable. We show that a natural candidate, the centered token log-probability increment $\log p(w_t)+H_t$, is the wrong observable for this purpose. Under the model's own sampling law it is a mean-zero martingale by construction, so it measures sampling self-consistency rather than trajectory health and is nearly silent during confident repetition, where both $\log p(w_t)$ and entropy are close to zero. We introduce a training-free decoding controller that combines (i) a degeneration-aware alarm score fusing token uncertainty with explicit verbatim repetition and (ii) a calibrated e-process-inspired sequential detector. The raw product process is Ville-valid under a conditional-mean null, while the deployed CUSUM-floored statistic is treated as an empirical change detector because the score is history-dependent and autocorrelated. On GSM8K with DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B in FP16 and INT4, calibration turns a monitor that fires on 93--95% of generations into a selective detector of failing traces ($φ\approx 0.3$, precision $\approx 0.6$ against a 0.38 base rate). In this pilot, the controller reduces measured verbatim-degeneration signals and yields a positive but statistically inconclusive INT4 accuracy change from 63% to 69% (paired McNemar $p=0.18$, $n=100$), at a 28% token-budget cost. We also find that non-termination, rather than looping, is the dominant failure mode on GSM8K. The main contribution is methodological: an explanation of why centered token log-probability is inadequate for decoder monitoring and a calibrated, cautiously evaluated replacement.

</details>


### [190] [Verifier-Guided Twelve-Tone Composition: A Generate-Verify-Repair Harness for Symbolic Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11334)
*Congren Dai,Danni Zhao,Enyang Liu,Michael Ching Yam,Zhancheng Guo,Siyi Gu,Wentao Yang,Bo Dai,Xiaobing Li,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号混合框架，将语言模型生成器与符号验证器结合在生成-验证-修复-追踪循环中，显著提升了十二音序列音乐生成的局部一致性和合法性，同时通过明确弃权机制避免输出非法结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成十二音序列乐谱时，虽然表面看似合法，但往往退化为混乱无序的纹理，缺乏真正的序列一致性。需要一种方法在保持生成能力的同时确保结构合法性。

Method: 采用神经符号混合架构，包装语言模型提议器，运行在"生成-验证-修复-追踪"循环中，结合符号验证器进行结构化检查。当无法通过验证时明确弃权而不输出。

Result: 在40个控制任务和4组配对模型上，经过审计的交付率从原始生成的13.3%提升至48.1%；更严格的碰撞与序列一致性检查通过率从33.5%提升至58.3%；退化率保持在0.05附近；对抗性提示下仍保持稳健。五位专家的盲评显示混合框架在遵循性、感知合法性、连贯性和整体质量方面均优于原始生成。

Conclusion: 神经符号混合框架能有效提升大语言模型生成十二音序列音乐的局部一致性和结构合法性，但尚未能保证整部作品完全合法，未来可在此基础上进一步扩展。

Abstract: Large language models can produce superficially legal twelve-tone scores that collapse into degenerate textures. We introduce a neuro-symbolic harness that wraps a language-model proposer in a generate-verify-repair-trace loop with symbolic verification. The complete pipeline improves event-local consistency without claiming whole-piece legality. Across 40 controlled tasks and four paired models, audited delivery yield rises from 13.3% under raw generation to 48.1% with the harness, which explicitly abstains otherwise. The pass rate of a narrower collision and serialisation-consistency check rises from 33.5% to 58.3%, while degeneracy remains near 0.05, including under exploratory adversarial prompting. A blinded evaluation by five experts also shows a descriptive aggregate preference for harness candidates over raw generation in adherence, perceived legality, coherence, and overall quality.

</details>


### [191] [AutoVSR: Automatic Visual-to-Symbolic Reasoning for Symbolic Expression Generation from Circuit Schematic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11338)
*Zhe Xiao,Longfei Li,Xu He,Haoying Wu,Zixing Zhang,Mingyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoVSR是一个利用视觉语言模型（VLM）从电路图自动生成符号表达式的框架。它通过将电路图重建为可执行中间表示（Executable IR），并借助符号求解器进行多步推导，相比端到端VLM和专用方法，准确率分别提升30.01-59.45%和41.96-51.84%，且计算效率优于闭源SOTA VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从电路图直接推导符号表达式具有挑战性，需要准确的视觉到符号的电路结构构建和正确的多步符号推导，现有方法难以同时满足这两点严格正确性要求。

Method: AutoVSR包含两个核心创新：(1) 基于组件规则检索和验证反馈引导的中间表示（Executable IR）构建方法；(2) 一个配备符号工具库的规划代理作为符号求解器，实现可靠的多步推导。

Result: 在主要符号表达式生成任务上，相比端到端VLM方法准确率提升30.01-59.45%，相比专用方法提升41.96-51.84%，且推理成本和计算效率优于闭源SOTA VLM。

Conclusion: AutoVSR通过视觉-符号结合的方式，有效解决了从电路图生成符号表达式的准确性问题，在性能和效率上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Symbolic expressions can effectively characterize and predict circuit behavior, but deriving them directly from circuit schematics is challenging. This process requires accurate visual-to-symbolic construction of circuit structure from images and correct multi-step symbolic derivation, both of which impose strict correctness requirements. This work proposes AutoVSR, an automated framework for visual-to-symbolic generation of circuit expressions using Vision Language Models (VLMs). By reconstructing circuit diagrams into an executable intermediate representation (Executable IR) and leveraging a symbolic solver for reasoning, AutoVSR significantly improves the accuracy of symbolic expression generation. AutoVSR introduces two key innovations: an IR construction method guided by component rule retrieval and verification-based feedback, and a symbolic solver implemented as a planning agent equipped with a symbolic tool library for reliable multi-step derivation. Compared with end-to-end VLM approaches and specialized methods on the main symbolic expression generation task, AutoVSR achieves accuracy improvements of 30.01--59.45% and 41.96--51.84%, respectively. Moreover, AutoVSR surpasses closed-source state-of-the-art VLMs in inference cost and computational efficiency. Code is available at https://github.com/LongfeiLi1/AutoVSR.

</details>


### [192] [Compile, Then Page: Executable SOP Programs and a Capability-Gated Runtime for Procedural LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11346)
*Chenglin Yu,Li Yin,Ying Yu,Hongxia Yang,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过将SOP约束编译为可执行伪代码，并结合程序引导的栈机器（LLM作为语义执行引擎），提出了一种增强企业智能体长期、条件性、安全关键型SOP遵循能力的方法。在SOPBench上的实验表明，编译表示优于官方文本（最高提升16.0分），运行时引导（如活动帧分页）仅在强模型上有效（正对比75:19 vs 58:31），弱模型反而受损。全程序消融实验表明，活动帧优先可恢复大部分拒绝增益，选择性可见性贡献较小。探测与审计分析表明，性能差异源自自发性状态纪律而非重建能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业智能体需要遵循长时域、条件性、安全关键的SOP，但现有LLM在直接理解自然语言描述的SOP时存在表现不稳定、易违反约束等问题，亟需更可靠的表示与执行机制。

Method: 将机器可读的SOP约束编译为可执行伪代码，采用程序引导的栈机器运行，LLM负责语义执行。通过活动帧分页机制在运行时提供结构化引导。设计了SOPBench进行三臂实验（原始文本/编译文本/程序引导），在6个模型上对比。

Result: 编译文本从不显著降低性能，在官方文本表现不佳时最高提升16.0分。运行时引导呈能力门控：强模型上正对比显著（75:19 vs 58:31），弱模型则受损。全程序消融恢复大部分强模型拒绝增益。Bank基准上三臂从70.4升至86.4再至92.8，拒绝正确率100%。

Conclusion: 实践建议：优先使用编译表示；仅在模型通过状态纪律检测后启用活动帧分页。性能差异主要由自发性状态纪律驱动，而非重建能力。

Abstract: Enterprise agents must follow long-horizon, conditional, safety-critical standard operating procedures (SOPs). We compile machine-readable SOP constraints into executable pseudo-code and run them with a program-guided (PG) stack machine that pages the active frame while an LLM performs semantic execution. A three-arm SOPBench study across six models separates representation from runtime: compiled text never significantly hurts and gains up to 16.0 points where official prose underperforms. Runtime guidance is capability-gated. Two strong models independently show positive seven-domain PG contrasts (58:19 and 75:31 discordant pairs), whereas weak models are harmed. A full-program cursor ablation (active frame first, complete program retained) recovers much of the strong-model refusal gain; selective visibility adds a smaller improvement. Paired probe and audit measurements track this divide to spontaneous state discipline rather than reconstruction ability. On Bank the three primary arms rise from 70.4 to 86.4 to 92.8, with 100% refusal correctness. Practical guidance: compile first; enable active-frame paging only after a model-level discipline check.

</details>


### [193] [From Neural Network Decisions to Training Cases: An Exact Account via Case-Based Decision Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11347)
*Manli Yan,Yuebin Lin,Yaowen Yu,Yong Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于OLS探针的案例级决策审计方法，通过在固定神经网络表示上拟合线性层，将每个动作分数精确分解为训练案例收益的加权和，权重由经验Gram几何决定。该方法可在不重训练或访问原始优化轨迹的情况下，提供案例级偏好结构、动作一致性及弱支持检测，在多个任务上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险决策场景（如医疗诊断、信用审批）需要案例级的审计证据，即哪些训练案例支持了某项决策及其结果。现有方法缺乏这种细粒度的可解释性。

Method: 在固定神经网络表示上拟合OLS线性探针（顶层读出层），将每个动作分数分解为训练案例收益的加权和，权重由经验Gram矩阵几何决定。识别了满足CBDT相似性语义的充分条件，超出该条件则权重视为带符号的Gram几何影响。仅需拟合顶层探针，无需重训练或访问原始优化轨迹。

Result: 在合成CBDT、PJM、Adult Income、Default Credit等任务上，该方法恢复了案例级偏好结构，在Top-30一致性指标上达到最高均值，支持重构性能与基线相当。探针保真度通过分数重构误差衡量。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种无需重训练或访问原始优化轨迹的案例级审计手段，可追溯分数来源、衡量动作一致性并识别弱支持案例，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Neural networks increasingly guide decisions in high-stakes domains such as medical diagnosis, credit approval, and energy bidding. Audit in these settings requires case-level evidence: which training cases support an action and what outcomes they carried. Case-based decision theory (CBDT) formalizes this reasoning by aggregating outcome support from remembered cases. We show that an OLS action readout fitted on a fixed neural representation admits an exact case-based decomposition. Each action score is a weighted sum of training-case returns, with coefficients determined by empirical Gram geometry. We identify a sufficient regime for CBDT similarity semantics; outside it, the coefficients should generally be treated as signed Gram-geometric influence. The decomposition yields audit signals that trace scores to training cases, measure action coherence, and identify weak support. Across synthetic CBDT, PJM, Adult Income, and Default Credit tasks, the method recovers case-level preference structure and achieves the highest mean Top-30 consistency among compared attribution baselines, while remaining competitive on support reconstruction. The audit requires only fitting an OLS top-layer probe, without retraining the representation or accessing the original optimization trajectory; probe fidelity is measured by score reconstruction.

</details>


### [194] [OpsMem: Dual-Memory Reasoning with Cross-Memory Resonance for Failure Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11357)
*Yongqian Sun,Rongchen Gao,Yu Luo,Wenwei Gu,Shenglin Zhang,Qingyi Guo,Qiuai Fu,Yaoliang Wu,Dan Pei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpsMem是一个双记忆框架，通过短期记忆维护当前诊断状态、长期记忆存储可复用运维经验，并利用跨记忆共振机制协调二者，在微服务故障诊断中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的诊断方法虽具备智能推理或知识增强能力，但缺乏在迭代诊断过程中协调不断演化的诊断状态与运维经验的机制。

Method: 提出OpsMem双记忆框架：短期记忆维护当前诊断状态，长期记忆存储可复用运维经验；通过跨记忆共振激活与状态相关的长期记忆；基于短期记忆和激活的长期记忆条件化多智能体诊断；从已解决的故障中整合可复用经验回写至长期记忆。

Result: 在华为真实微服务故障诊断数据集上，OpsMem在Match和Relevant指标上分别比最强基线提升46.88%和18.39%。

Conclusion: OpsMem通过双记忆架构和跨记忆共振机制，有效协调诊断状态与运维经验，显著优于现有智能推理和知识增强方法。

Abstract: Failure diagnosis in modern software systems requires iterative evidence acquisition and hypothesis reasoning guided by operational experience. Existing LLM-based methods improve diagnosis through agentic reasoning or knowledge augmentation, but they often lack a mechanism to coordinate the evolving diagnostic state with operational experience during iterative diagnosis. We propose OpsMem, a dual-memory framework that maintains a short-term memory for the current diagnostic state and a long-term memory for reusable operational experience. OpsMem uses cross-memory resonance to activate state-relevant long-term memory, conditions multi-agent diagnosis on the short-term and activated long-term memories, and consolidates reusable experience from solved incidents back into long-term memory. Experiments on a real-world Huawei microservice failure diagnosis dataset show that OpsMem outperforms representative agentic-reasoning and knowledge-augmented baselines, improving Match and Relevant by up to 46.88% and 18.39% over the strongest baseline, respectively.

</details>


### [195] [StructAgent: Harness Long-horizon Digital Agents with Unified Causal Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11388)
*Wenyi Wu,Sibo Zhu,Kun Zhou,Aayush Salvi,Zixuan Song,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StructAgent通过引入统一的因果任务状态表示和基于验证器的工作流结构，显著提升了LLM/VLM在长期计算机使用任务中的可靠性，在OSWorld-Verified上将Qwen3.5-9B成功率从27.0%提升至46.9%，Qwen3.5-27B从31.6%提升至62.2%，并以MiniMax-M3达到78.9%的新开源SOTA，且框架可泛化至Minecraft环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字智能体通常基于原始交互历史进行操作，导致任务进度难以解释、验证和恢复，限制了在长期任务中的可靠执行能力。本文认为解决该问题需要显式地围绕统一的任务进度因果表示来构建智能体的状态和工作流。

Method: 提出StructAgent，一个以状态为中心的框架，包含：(1) 统一状态，用于维护紧凑且可验证的任务进度；(2) 结构化工作流，通过基于验证器的状态转换来规范进度推进；(3) 显式进度检查点、证据驱动的任务完成、定向失败恢复和工具支持执行，且所有进度更新均经过验证。

Result: 在OSWorld-Verified上，Qwen3.5-9B从27.0%提升至46.9%，Qwen3.5-27B从31.6%提升至62.2%，MiniMax-M3达到78.9%的新开源SOTA。该框架同样适用于Minecraft环境，证明了其通用性。

Conclusion: 显式结构化任务进度表示和基于验证的工作流是提升长期计算机使用任务中智能体可靠性的有效范式，且该设计具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) have enabled increasingly capable digital agents for computer use. However, real-world tasks are often long-horizon and involve evolving contexts containing accumulated observations, intermediate edits, failed attempts, and partially completed executions. Existing agents typically operate over raw interaction history, making task progress difficult to interpret, verify, and recover, which ultimately limits reliable long-horizon execution. In this paper, we argue that addressing this challenge requires explicitly structuring both the agent's state and workflow around a unified causal representation of task progress. We present \textbf{StructAgent}, a state-centered framework that introduces a unified state for maintaining compact, verifiable task progress and a structured workflow that regulates progress through verifier-backed state transitions. Building on this design, StructAgent further enables explicit progress checkpointing, evidence-driven task completion, targeted failure recovery, and tool-supported execution, while ensuring that all progress updates remain grounded in verification. Extensive experiments demonstrate that StructAgent consistently improves a wide range of LLM and VLM backbones on long-horizon computer-use tasks. On OSWorld-Verified, it improves Qwen3.5-9B from 27.0\% to 46.9\% success rate and Qwen3.5-27B from 31.6\% to 62.2\%, while achieving a new open-source state of the art of 78.9\% with MiniMax-M3. Moreover, the same framework generalizes beyond desktop environments to Minecraft, demonstrating the generality of our design.

</details>


### [196] [Omni-Decision: A Progressive Evidence-State Agent System for Omni-Modal QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11433)
*Ming Ma,Yi Zhu,Yiran Zhong,Feida Zhu,Weigao Sun,Junhan Shi,Lingrui Mei,Tianming Yang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Omni-Decision是一种无需训练的、基于证据状态显式管理的全模态问答系统，通过结构化证据状态引导规划、采集、验证与修复，在OmniGAIA和WorldSense上分别提升27.3和30.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态智能体系统将证据零散地置于草稿板、工具轨迹或自由形式历史中，难以追踪哪些证据已被确认、哪些仍然缺失，以及何时证据足以回答问题，亟需一种显式的证据状态管理机制。

Method: 提出Omni-Decision训练无关系统，将全模态问答转化为查询范围的证据闭合过程。为每个查询维护结构化的证据状态（已确认证据、未解决冲突、事实与计算依赖、开放证据需求），共享状态视图条件化规划、证据采集、验证、修复和最终化。来自媒体、网页、计算和验证模块的异构观测经归一化、判断并提交为确定性状态更新。

Result: OmniGAIA上45.6%准确率，WorldSense上58.3%准确率，分别超过基线27.3和30.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式证据状态控制对多步全模态证据寻求任务至关重要，消融实验和轨迹审计进一步验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Omni-modal evidence-seeking QA requires agents to answer questions whose evidence is sparsely distributed across videos, audio, images, web pages, and computation results. Existing agentic multimodal systems often leave evidence in scratchpads, tool trajectories, or free-form histories, making it difficult to track what has been grounded, what remains missing, and when the evidence is sufficient to answer. We propose Omni-Decision, a training-free evidence-state system that turns omni-modal QA into a query-scoped evidence-closure process. For each query, Omni-Decision maintains a structured evidence state containing confirmed evidence, unresolved conflicts, fact and computation dependencies, and open evidence needs. A shared state view conditions planning, evidence acquisition, validation, repair, and finalization. Heterogeneous observations from media, web, computation, and verification modules are normalized, judged, and committed through deterministic state updates. This design enables targeted evidence acquisition, preserves sparse cross-modal cues, and provides inspectable control over repair and stopping. Omni-Decision achieves 45.6% accuracy on OmniGAIA and 58.3% on WorldSense, improving over the baselines by +27.3 and +30.2 percentage points, respectively. No-state ablations and trajectory audits further support the role of explicit evidence-state control in multi-step omni-modal evidence seeking.

</details>


### [197] [The Ebb and Flow of Multimodal Focus: Scheduling Visual Relay Windows for Grounded VLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11436)
*Wencheng Ye,Yi Bin,Yujuan Ding,Hongye Fang,Zheng Wang,Xing Xu,Jingkuan Song,Yun Zhang,Sirui Da,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过机制分析发现VLM中存在稳定的三阶段注意力重分布模式，其中间阶段为视觉中继窗口（VRW），并据此提出TRACE框架，通过任务自适应推理时控制来增强多模态推理中的证据基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型在多模态推理基准上表现越来越好，但其视觉证据进入语言堆栈后往往变得不稳定，削弱了基于证据的推理能力。作者希望理解这种脆弱性的内部机制。

Method: 首先通过机制分析揭示VLM内部存在三阶段注意力重分布模式：早期问题条件组织、中间视觉主导中继、晚期答案形成。将中间阶段定义为视觉中继窗口（VRW）。基于此提出TRACE框架，包含轻量训练模块，在预填充阶段重塑中继分配，在解码阶段保留组装的视觉支持。

Result: 在4个开源VLM骨干和7个基准测试上，TRACE在接地敏感设置上平均提升4.33分，最高提升6.6分，同时推理密集型任务也有改进。

Conclusion: 显式控制跨深度的多模态注意力焦点为增强基于证据的多模态推理提供了统一且有效的机制。

Abstract: Vision-language models increasingly succeed on multimodal reasoning benchmarks, yet their visual evidence often becomes unstable once it enters the language stack, weakening evidence-grounded reasoning. To understand this fragility, we examine the internal dynamics of VLMs through a mechanistic lens and uncover a stable three-stage redistribution of multimodal attention focus across depth: an early question-conditioned organization, a critical middle visual-dominant relay, and a late return to answer formation. We operationalize the middle phase as the Visual Relay Window (VRW), and show that its geometry varies with task demand, is causally tied to grounded generation, and distinguishes unsupported answers from stronger reasoning trajectories. Guided by this internal rhythm, we propose TRACE, a task-adaptive inference-time control framework with lightweight trained modules. It reshapes relay allocation during prefill and preserves assembled visual support after handoff during decoding. Across four open-weight VLM backbones and seven benchmarks, TRACE delivers large gains on grounding-sensitive settings, improving them by 4.33 points on average and by up to 6.6 points, while also improving reasoning-heavy tasks. These results show that explicitly controlling multimodal focus across depth offers a unified and effective mechanism for strengthening evidence-grounded multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [198] [Enhancing Query Efficiency for d-DNNF Representations Through Preprocessing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11492)
*Jean Marie Lagniez,Emmanuel Lonca*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了面向CNF公式的预处理技术，发现保持模型计数的预处理器可以有效提升均匀采样、直接模型访问和模型枚举三种任务的效率，实验表明预处理方法在对CNF编译为d-DNNF表示时表现稳健且高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数最先进的预处理器不保持公式等价性，因此不适用于模型访问任务（均匀采样、直接模型访问、模型枚举），亟需探索能够有效支持这些任务的预处理方法。

Method: 分析多种预处理技术的特性，聚焦于保持模型计数的预处理器，并在多个领域的基准测试上进行实验验证，将CNF公式编译为d-DNNF表示进行模型访问查询。

Result: 提出的预处理方法在不同领域的基准测试中表现出高效性和鲁棒性，在将CNF公式编译为d-DNNF表示时，模型访问查询的性能得到显著提升。

Conclusion: 保持模型计数的预处理方法结合适当的预处理信息维护，可以有效提升模型访问任务的效率，为解决该问题提供了可行的技术路线。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate preprocessing techniques aimed at improving the efficiency of accessing models of propositional formulas represented in conjunctive normal form (CNF). We focus on three fundamental tasks: uniform sampling, direct model access, and model enumeration. Our analysis reveals that most state-of-the-art preprocessors, when they do not preserve formula equivalence, are generally unsuitable for these tasks. In contrast, we demonstrate that preprocessors which preserve model counts can be effectively leveraged, provided relevant preprocessing information is maintained. To validate our approach, we perform extensive experiments on a diverse suite of benchmarks from multiple domains. The experimental results show that our preprocessing methods are both efficient and robust, yielding significant performance improvements for model access queries when CNF formulas are compiled into d-DNNF representations.

</details>


### [199] [Comparative Analysis of GAT and BERT for Human-Like Playtesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11501)
*Kleio Fragkedaki,Theodoros Panagiotakopoulos,Matteo Biasielli,Hui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了两种通用网络架构（Transformer的BERT和图注意力GAT）来建模游戏板关系结构，相比CNN基线在《糖果粉碎传奇》中取得了更好的性能，减少了对特征工程的持续维护需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法难以捕捉玩家全部策略，且需要大量特征工程和网络架构调整，尤其是在引入新游戏机制或功能时，模型需不断调整，缺乏泛化能力。

Method: 提出两种通用网络架构：（a）基于Transformer的BERT模型和（b）图注意力模型（GAT），用于捕捉《糖果粉碎传奇》游戏板的关系结构，并与CNN基线进行对比实验。

Result: 所提出的BERT和GAT模型在挑战性游戏板配置上优于CNN基线，展现了更好的泛化性能和通用表示能力。

Conclusion: 通用网络架构能够减少甚至消除对持续特征工程维护的需求，为游戏体验预测建模提供了更具泛化性的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurately modeling and understanding player experience is crucial for designing engaging puzzle games. To achieve this, a common approach involves collecting diverse user data to train predictive playtesting models that mimic player behavior. However, existing data-driven methods often lack the ability to capture the full range of player strategies and require extensive feature engineering and network architecture modeling. This limitation becomes particularly evident when new game mechanics or features are introduced, which necessitate continual adjustments to the models. To addrss these challenges, we propose a more generalized representation that reduces - or even eliminates - the need for ongoing feature-engineering maintenance. Specifically, we investigate two general-purpose network architectures: (a) a transformer-based model (BERT) and (b) a graph attention model (GAT), both of which are designed to effectively capture the relational structure of Candy Crush Saga (CCS) game boards. Our experiments compare these approaches to Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN) baselines, revealing better performance on challenging board configurations and underscoring the benefits of our generalizable representation.

</details>


### [200] [Learning Residual Kinematic Corrections for Continuous Neural Decoding via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11530)
*Jiamian Li,Niall McShane,Attila Korik,Naomi du Bois,Karl McCreadie,Leen Jabban,Benjamin Metcalfe,Özgür Şimşek,Damien Coyle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段解码框架（CNN-LSTM-RL），通过强化学习对CNN-LSTM输出的运动想象轨迹进行残差校正，在2D和VR环境下分别将相关系数提升41.5%和21.2%，RMSE降低40.2%和38.2%，无需额外神经数据即可提升三维脑机接口解码精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于非侵入式EEG的连续三维运动想象解码仍面临信号变异性大、残差解码误差持续存在的问题，现有CNN-LSTM等深度学习架构虽能捕捉时空动态，但预测轨迹中存在系统性残差误差。

Method: 提出两阶段解码框架：第一阶段用CNN-LSTM解码器生成初始运动学轨迹；第二阶段训练一个强化学习(RL)智能体对预测轨迹进行残差校正（CNN-LSTM-RL），RL在离线状态下训练，无需直接输入EEG信号，而是优化预测轨迹与目标轨迹之间的运动精度。

Result: 在2D环境下，平均相关系数从0.5076提升至0.7181（p=0.0005），提升41.5%；RMSE从0.0890降至0.0532（p<0.0001），降低40.2%。在VR环境下，平均相关系数从0.6420提升至0.7780（p=0.0059），提升21.2%；RMSE从0.0714降至0.0441（p<0.0001），降低38.2%。

Conclusion: 该可扩展框架通过离线残差RL校正运动学误差，无需额外神经数据即可显著提升三维BCI运动想象解码性能，对神经康复、假肢控制和虚拟交互领域具有推进意义。

Abstract: Decoding continuous three-dimensional (3D) motor imagery (MI) using non-invasive electroencephalography (EEG)-based brain--computer interfaces (BCIs) remains challenging due to signal variability and residual decoding errors. Deep learning architectures such as convolutional neural network--long short-term memory (CNN--LSTM) models can capture spatial and temporal dynamics for continuous kinematic decoding; however, systematic residual errors persist in predicted trajectories. We propose a two-stage decoding framework that applies reinforcement learning (RL) to perform residual kinematic correction on the outputs of a CNN--LSTM decoder (CNN--LSTM--RL). The RL agent is trained offline without direct EEG input and instead operates on predicted kinematic trajectories to optimize movement accuracy relative to target trajectories. Decoding performance was quantified using Pearson correlation coefficients ($r$) and Root Mean Square Errors (RMSE) along the $x, y$, and $z$ axes. Compared to CNN--LSTM applied alone, CNN--LSTM--RL improved the mean correlation from $0.5076$ to $0.7181$ ($p = 0.0005$) in 2D and from $0.6420$ to $0.7780$ ($p = 0.0059$) in VR, with relative gains of $41.5\%$ and $21.2\%$, respectively. Correspondingly, RMSE was reduced from $0.0890$ to $0.0532$ (2D, $p < 0.0001$) and from $0.0714$ to $0.0441$ (VR, $p < 0.0001$), representing relative reductions of $40.2\%$ and $38.2\%$. These findings demonstrate that this scalable framework enhances 3D BCI MI decoding by correcting kinematic errors via offline residual RL without extra neural data, advancing neurorehabilitation, prosthetics, and virtual interaction.

</details>


### [201] [HCRMap: Pressure-Aware Hot-Expert Residency Mapping for 3.5D MoE Chiplet Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11586)
*Yongqin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HCRMap提出了一种面向3.5D多芯片系统的热专家驻留映射框架，通过动态管理专家副本在不同存储层级间的放置与重用，联合缓解通信、内存和队列瓶颈，在预填充和解码阶段分别降低端到端延迟43.6%和43.0%（相比Hydra）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE大语言模型在推理时令牌路由导致专家热点倾斜问题：少数热专家持续接收大多数令牌，而其余专家负载轻。在3.5D多芯片系统上，这种倾斜不仅造成计算不平衡，还加剧了通信、内存带宽、I/O和执行队列的压力。核心问题并非简单地减少令牌移动，而是需要在不同存储层级间动态放置和复用热专家副本。

Method: HCRMap框架基于专家热度、权重加载成本、迁移开销和运行时资源压力，动态决定专家应被提升、保留、降级或驱逐，并将路由令牌组映射到合适的驻留副本，从而联合缓解通信、内存和队列瓶颈。

Result: 相比Hydra在预填充和解码阶段端到端延迟分别降低43.6%和43.0%；相比MoEntwine分别降低34.5%和33.1%；相比PIMoE分别降低46.7%和46.0%。

Conclusion: HCRMap通过压力感知的专家副本管理方案，有效解决了3.5D MoE推理中的专家热点倾斜和资源瓶颈问题，显著降低了端到端推理延迟。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) large language models (LLM) activate only a small number of experts during inference, but token routing introduces persistent expert hotness skew: a small set of hot experts continuously receives most tokens, while the remaining experts are lightly loaded. On 3.5D multi-chiplet systems, this skew not only causes compute imbalance but also amplifies pressure on communication, memory bandwidth, I/O, and execution queues. Therefore, the core problem is not simply to reduce token movement, but to dynamically place and reuse hot expert replicas across different memory tiers.
  This paper proposes HCRMap, a hot expert residency mapping framework for pressure-aware expert replica management in 3.5D MoE inference. Based on expert hotness, weight loading cost, migration overhead, and runtime resource pressure, HCRMap dynamically determines which experts should be promoted, retained, demoted, or evicted. It then maps routed token groups to suitable resident replicas, thereby jointly mitigating communication, memory, and queue bottlenecks.
  Experimental results show that HCRMap reduces end-to-end latency by 43.6% and 43.0% over Hydra in the prefill and decode stages, respectively; by 34.5% and 33.1% over MoEntwine; and by 46.7% and 46.0% over PIMoE.

</details>


### [202] [Lesioned Multimodal Language Models Reproduce Aphasic Picture-Naming Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11621)
*Yong Yang,Xiang Guan,Sophie Arheix-Parras,Saeed Ahmadi,Roger Newman-Norlund,Leonardo Bonilha,Christopher Rorden,Julius Fridriksson,Rutvik H. Desai,Srihari Nelakuditi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过对多模态语言模型LLaVA 1.6施加扰动，成功复现了卒中后失语症患者在图片命名任务中的个体化错误模式，97.8%的患者在至少六个错误类别上匹配，79.5%的患者七个类别全部匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究通用语言模型是否能够复现卒中后失语症患者特有的系统性命名错误模式，从而为建立患者的数字孪生提供可能。

Method: 使用LLaVA 1.6模型，通过改变扰动层、比例和噪声量等配置，对模型单元施加控制性扰动；采用经过验证的神经分类器将278名失语症患者的费城命名测试反应分为七类，并与模型输出进行比较。

Result: 除形式性错语外，六个错误类别（正确、语义、混合、无关、新语、无反应）在模型的不同参数空间区域以临床可比的比例出现；搜索扰动空间后，97.8%的患者在至少六个类别上匹配个体错误谱，79.5%的患者七个类别全部匹配；蒙特卡洛基线验证表明匹配反映了联合跨类别结构。

Conclusion: 建立了复现失语症患者图片命名个体化错误模式的量化框架，表明语言模型有潜力作为卒中后失语症个体的数字孪生。

Abstract: Aphasia following stroke commonly produces systematic naming errors with characteristic profiles, but whether general-purpose language models not designed for clinical simulation can reproduce these patterns remains untested. We investigated (1) whether lesions or controlled perturbations to a multimodal language model can reproduce different types of errors in picture naming, and (2) whether the framework can reproduce the complete error profile of individual persons with aphasia (PWAs). Using LLaVA 1.6, we evaluated perturbation configurations that varied the layer, proportion, and amount of noise applied to model units. We examined 278 PWAs on the Philadelphia Naming Test, classifying responses into seven categories using a validated neural classifier. Six of seven response categories (correct, semantic, mixed, unrelated, neologism, no response errors) emerged at clinically-comparable proportions across distinct parameter space regions, with formal paraphasia being the exception. Searching the perturbation space revealed configurations that reproduced the individual error profile in at least six of seven categories for 97.8% of PWAs and in all seven categories for 79.5% of PWAs. Monte Carlo baselines confirmed that this matching reflects joint inter-category structure rather than marginal overlap. These results establish a quantitative framework for reproducing individual aphasic error patterns in picture naming. They suggest the potential for language models to serve as digital twins of individuals with post-stroke aphasia.

</details>


### [203] [MAGIC: Transition-Aware Generation of Navigable Multi-Scene Game Worlds with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11594)
*Tsz Hei Fan,Choi Wing Fung,Yuxuan Wan,Shuqing Li,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAGIC是一个提示到项目的四阶段流水线系统，能够将自然语言提示转换为可运行的多场景游戏项目，解决了跨场景一致性、场景内可导航性和过渡评估三大问题，在100个多场景基准测试中实现了0.99精确率、0.95召回率和0.96 F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代3D游戏中的多场景导航创作非常耗时，每个传送门需要一致的端点、每个内部空间在布置后仍需保持可导航性，且跨文件的连接一致性难以维护。现有LLM/MLLM场景生成器虽降低了单室内合成成本，但一次只生成一个场景，无法通过简单重复获得连贯的多场景世界。

Method: MAGIC是一个四阶段流水线：(1) 规划共享的过渡感知中间表示；(2) 使用洪泛填充验证器确保传送门可达性来指定每个场景；(3) 生成场景及其过渡脚本；(4) 将它们组合成一个项目。同时引入了一个过渡聚焦评估代理来执行每个过渡。

Result: 在100个多场景案例的基准测试中，MAGIC为每个案例生成了可执行项目，端到端过渡识别达到0.99精确率、0.95召回率和0.96 F1分数。逐阶段比较中，它比LLM基线和Holodeck恢复了更多真实传送门并生成了更可导航的布局。

Conclusion: MAGIC有效解决了多场景导航游戏创作中的跨场景一致性、场景内可导航性和过渡评估三大核心障碍，是首个将自然语言提示直接转化为可运行多场景游戏项目的端到端系统。

Abstract: Multi-scene navigation (clearing an objective in one bounded space and then crossing a portal into the next) is a defining feature of contemporary 3D games, but authoring it is laborious: every portal must have consistent endpoints on both sides, each interior must remain navigable once it is furnished, and the resulting connectivity must be kept consistent across many files. Recent large language model (LLM) and multimodal LLM (MLLM) scene generators have made single-interior synthesis dramatically cheaper, yet they produce one scene at a time and cannot, by naive repetition, yield a connected multi-scene world. We identify three obstacles that single-scene methods leave unsolved: cross-scene consistency, in-scene navigability, and the evaluation of whether a transition actually works. We present MAGIC, a prompt-to-project system that addresses all three. MAGIC is a four-stage pipeline that turns a single natural-language prompt into a runnable multi-scene game project: it plans a shared transition-aware intermediate representation, specifies each scene while enforcing portal reachability with a flood-fill validator, generates the scenes together with their transition scripts, and combines them into one project. Because existing single-scene fidelity metrics never execute a transition, we further introduce a transition-focused evaluation agent that runs each transition in play. On a new benchmark of 100 multi-scene cases, MAGIC produces an executable project for every case and reaches 0.99 precision, 0.95 recall, and 0.96 F1 on end-to-end transition identification; stage by stage, it recovers more ground-truth portals and yields markedly more navigable layouts than an LLM baseline and Holodeck. Our code is available at https://github.com/sereneee1201/MAGIC/.

</details>


### [204] [Reproducing human biases in route choice using large language models: Toward scalable behavioral modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11632)
*Jiangtao Han,Shoufeng Ma,Shuxian Xu,Geng Li,Shuai Ling,Ning Jia,Zhengbing He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）能否在没有显式指定前景理论参数的情况下，复现人类在选择行为中的非理性偏差。以路径选择为场景，实验表明LLM能够再现与前景理论一致的人类决策偏差，为大规模智能体建模提供了可扩展的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前景理论（CPT）能有效刻画人类决策偏差，但其大规模应用（如仿真和基于智能体的建模）依赖于个体级CPT参数的标定，传统方法（调查和受控实验）难以泛化且无法捕捉人类决策的多样性。

Method: 以路径选择为代表性场景，设计行为评估框架，系统比较LLM生成的决策与CPT预测的人类行为模式。

Result: LLM能够复现非理性的人类选择偏差，并在不确定性下展现与前景理论效应一致的决策行为。

Conclusion: 生成式AI模型为建模人类决策过程提供了可扩展的替代方案，为下一代大规模智能体仿真和AI驱动的行为研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Human choice behavior, including route choice, exhibits systematic behavioral biases that deviate from the assumptions of full rationality. Cumulative prospect theory (CPT) has been widely recognized as an effective framework for characterizing such behavioral patterns. However, its large-scale application, particularly in simulation and agent-based modeling, critically depends on specifying individual-level CPT parameters, which remain a major bottleneck. Conventional approaches typically rely on surveys and controlled experiments to calibrate CPT parameters, yet these methods are difficult to generalize and often fail to capture the full diversity of human decision-making. To address this challenge, this paper investigates whether large language models (LLMs) can reproduce human behavioral biases in choice-making without explicit specification of prospect-theoretic parameters. Using route choice as a representative scenario, we design a behavioral evaluation framework and systematically compare LLM-generated decisions with established human behavioral patterns predicted by CPT. Experimental results demonstrate that LLMs are capable of reproducing non-rational human choice biases and can exhibit decision behaviors consistent with prospect-theoretic effects under uncertainty. These findings suggest that generative AI models may provide a scalable alternative for modeling human decision processes and offer a promising foundation for next-generation large-scale agent-based simulation and AI-driven behavioral research.

</details>


### [205] [Interaction Scaling: Grounding the Third Axis of Test-Time Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11598)
*Bojie Li,Noah Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出第三种测试时扩展方法——交互（Interaction）：模型生成工件，外部工具观察实际表现，模型据此修订。相比推理扩展和采样扩展，交互通过引入真实观测打破了前两者的性能天花板。关键在于"接地性"（Grounding）——反馈和评估都必须来自真实观察而非模型内部评判。在编程和视觉任务上验证了交互扩展的有效性，并指出非接地评估（如VLM看截图）会掩盖缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展方法（推理扩展和采样扩展）存在根本限制：所有额外计算都来自同一组冻结权重和同一提示，无法引入模型不知道的外部信息。这导致性能存在天花板。作者希望探索第三种方式——交互，通过引入真实世界观测来突破这一限制。

Method: 提出"接地性"（Grounding）概念，要求反馈和评估都来自真实观察。设计"提议者-评审者"（proposer-reviewer）框架进行交互式修订。在编程任务上，对比推理扩展、最佳N采样（即使有预言机选择最优样本）与多种交互策略；在视觉任务上，对比使用VLM看截图（非接地评估）与真实布局测量工具（接地评估）的交互效果。

Result: 编程任务：推理扩展和最佳N采样均出现平台期，而交互策略持续改进，提议者-评审者框架达到100%通过率且零方差，在三个模型族上一致。视觉任务：VLM看截图评分将14/15的明显缺陷评为"完美"（隐藏缺陷），而真实布局测量工具显示交互可减少40-74%的缺陷。使用同一VLM作为评审者时，幻灯片布局反而变差，而测量工具则修复了问题。

Conclusion: 交互扩展是真实且独立于推理和采样的第三维度，但只有反馈和评估都接地（即来自真实观测）时才能发挥效果。非接地的评估（如VLM看图）会掩盖缺陷，导致交互失效甚至恶化结果。

Abstract: There are two standard ways to spend more compute at test time: let a model reason longer, or sample more attempts and keep one. Both share a hidden limit: they are internal. Every extra token comes from the same frozen weights and the same prompt, so neither can tell the model anything it does not already know. We study a third way, interaction: the model proposes an artifact, an external instrument observes how it actually behaves, and the model revises. Each cycle imports a real observation, so interaction breaks through the ceiling the other two hit.
  We argue that a single variable governs this third axis, grounding, and that it must hold on both sides of the loop. The feedback that drives revision must come from an instrument that actually observes the flaw, and so must the metric that scores the result. On hard coding tasks at a fixed token budget, reasoning-only and best-of-N sampling both plateau (the latter even when an oracle picks the best sample), while every interaction strategy keeps improving; our proposer-reviewer harness reaches a perfect 100% pass rate with no run-to-run variance, and the gain holds across three model families. On rendered visual artifacts, the usual judge (a vision-language model, or VLM, reading a screenshot) rates 14 of 15 visibly broken figures "perfect," because the screenshot hides the flaws before the judge can see them. A tool that measures the real layout instead shows the loop removing 40-74% of defects across four modalities; and that same VLM, used as the reviewer, makes slide layouts worse where the measuring tool repairs them. Interaction scaling is real and distinct from reasoning and sampling, but only visible when both the feedback and the metric are grounded.

</details>


### [206] [Auditing the Risk Claims of Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11607)
*Hari Prasad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统审计了分布强化学习智能体（QR-DQN、C51、IQN）在MinAtar和Atari环境中学到的风险声称（如CVaR）。通过基于Wasserstein距离的决策相关筛选指标、snapshot-restart蒙特卡洛真值、置换检验、bootstrap反驳和FDR控制的统计框架，发现40-95%的最强风险权衡声明在95%置信度下被反驳，智能体声称的"风险"实为训练伪影而非环境随机性，且该伪影是结构性的（训练早期即形成、与最终得分不相关、每个种子特异），在Atari规模下同样存在。正面控制实验确认96-100%的真实声明，证明审计方法本身有效。采取智能体CVaR建议的行动可能显著差于随机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布RL智能体学到的完整回报分布正被用于风险敏感控制、安全监控等关键决策，其风险声称的可信度缺乏直接验证。理论虽预测分布RL的风险声称可能存在偏差，但尚未有工作直接测量训练后的智能体风险声称是否真实反映环境随机性。

Method: 1) 提出决策相关的筛选指标：前两个动作间的超额Wasserstein距离（等于一阶随机占优违反的质量）；2) 使用snapshot-restart蒙特卡洛获取环境真实风险；3) 构建统计验证框架：置换零假设、bootstrap反驳、FDR控制；4) 在QR-DQN、C51、IQN上对MinAtar（33次运行）和Atari进行审计；5) 设置正面控制实验验证审计方法有效性。

Result: 1) 40-95%的最强风险权衡声明在95%置信度下被反驳，最强声明的定位与盲目猜测统计上无区别；2) 几乎没有任何声明能被确认；3) 伪影是结构性的：训练早期完全形成、与最终分数不相关、每个种子特异；4) Atari规模上每个预训练的顶尖Breakout QR-DQN声明都被反驳；5) 正面控制确认96-100%的真实声明（相关性0.89-0.92）；6) 按CVaR建议行动可能显著差于随机；7) 风险训练或集成无法消除该伪影，重校准只有通过使声明无效才能通过审计。

Conclusion: 当前分布RL智能体学习到的风险声称不可靠，反映的是训练伪影而非环境随机性，这对其在风险敏感控制和安全性监测中的应用构成严重警示。头部是不提供信息的（uninformative），而不仅仅是校准不良的（miscalibrated）。研究还揭示了两个导致虚假审计结论的隐蔽陷阱，并发布了审计工具包。

Abstract: Distributional reinforcement learning agents learn full return distributions that are increasingly read at face value: for interpretability, risk-sensitive control, and safety monitoring. We ask a question theory anticipates but that has not been measured directly: are the risk claims of a trained distributional agent true? Our audit combines a decision-relevant screening metric (the excess Wasserstein gap between the top two actions, which equals the mass by which first-order stochastic dominance is violated), ground truth from snapshot-restart Monte Carlo, and a statistical harness (permutation nulls, bootstrap refutation, FDR control) without which the audit itself manufactures false conclusions. Across QR-DQN, C51, and IQN on MinAtar (33 runs), 40-95% of the strongest claimed risk trade-offs are refuted at 95% confidence, the placement of the strongest claims is statistically indistinguishable from truth-blind, and essentially no claim is confirmable: for these agents, the learned "risk" reflects a training artifact rather than environment stochasticity. The artifact is structural (fully formed early in training, uncorrelated with final score, idiosyncratic to each seed) and appears unchanged at full-Atari scale, with every top Breakout claim of a pretrained near-state-of-the-art QR-DQN refuted. Positive controls of known magnitude confirm 96-100% of real claims (correlation 0.89-0.92): the reading measures the agents, not the audit. Acting on the heads' CVaR advice at their most-flagged states ranges from beneficial to significantly worse than chance. Neither training for risk nor ensembling removes the artifact, and recalibration passes the audit only by nullifying the claims: the head is uninformative, not merely miscalibrated. We release the toolkit and document two silent pitfalls that produced convincing but wrong audits of our own.

</details>


### [207] [Think Through a Bottleneck: Hourglass Reasoning for Rigorous Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11696)
*Huan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"沙漏推理"（Hourglass reasoning）方法，通过严格隔离推理阶段、仅允许压缩符号状态（规则和模式）在阶段间传递，显著提升了大语言模型在小样本归纳推理中的表现。在ARC-AGI-2、ChipBench和BBEH-Linguini三个基准上，该方法相比迭代式基线取得了显著提升，最高提升达27个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，自我精炼（self-refinement）和显式规则陈述往往无法有效提升LLM的小样本归纳推理能力，甚至可能产生负面影响。作者认为关键在于推理阶段之间的信息流控制方式，而非使用的语言形式。

Method: 提出沙漏推理框架，由冻结的LLM作为元构建器，为每个任务构建一个符号编码器-解码器结构：(1) Induction模块将支持示例压缩为模式φ和临时脚手架z；(2) Deduction模块从这些信息推导规则T并丢弃z；(3) Implementer将(φ,T)编译为最终产物；(4) 错误驱动的Refiner修订(φ,T)并从头重新生成产物。关键约束是只有(φ,T)能跨越阶段边界，确保所有精炼都锚定在规则上。

Result: 在三个基准上取得显著提升：ARC-AGI-2上最佳5次准确率提升14个百分点；ChipBench上使用GPT-5.5将Verilog综合准确率从31%提升至58%（近乎翻倍）；BBEH-Linguini上使用Gemini 3.1 Pro完全逆转了显式规则表述带来的性能下降趋势。

Conclusion: 消融实验证实，性能提升来自阶段间的严格隔离和初始归纳的质量，而非提示措辞或特定的符号形式。在冻结LLM中，驱动归纳推理的是信息在推理过程中的流动方式，而非表达所用的语言。

Abstract: Self-refinement often fails to strengthen few-shot inductive reasoning in large language models. Prompting a model to explicitly state its inferred rule does little on its own. What actually matters is a structurally enforced isolation between reasoning stages, so that information can only pass between them as a compressed symbolic state.
  We introduce \textbf{Hourglass reasoning}, which enforces strict context isolation between reasoning stages. The frozen LLM acts as a meta-constructor, building for each task a symbolic encoder--decoder: an Induction module compresses the support examples into a schema $φ$ (encoder) and a transient scaffold $z$; a Deduction module derives rule $T$ (decoder) from these and discards $z$; an Implementer compiles $(φ, T)$ into artifacts; an error-driven Refiner revises $(φ, T)$ and regenerates artifacts from scratch. Only $(φ, T)$ crosses stage boundaries, so all refinement stays anchored to the rule.
  We evaluate Hourglass across three benchmarks spanning visual abstraction, hardware synthesis, and textual rule induction, using GPT-5.5 and Gemini 3.1 Pro. On ARC-AGI-2, it raises best-of-5 accuracy by up to 14 points over an iterative-refinement baseline. On ChipBench, it nearly doubles Verilog synthesis accuracy with GPT-5.5, from 31\% to 58\%. BBEH-Linguini draws on puzzles from the International Linguistics Olympiad, a setting where prior work has shown that explicit verbalization can hurt performance. Hourglass mitigates this tendency, and on Gemini 3.1 Pro, it reverses the effect entirely.
  Ablations confirm that these gains come from the isolation between stages and the quality of the initial induction, not from prompt wording or the particular symbolic form used. It is how information flows through the reasoning process, rather than the language used to express it, that drives inductive reasoning in frozen LLMs.

</details>


### [208] [Playful AI in Professional Email: A Field Experiment on Tone and Recipient Engagement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11749)
*Ziv Ben-Zion,Teddy Lazebnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过121名员工、16880封邮件的随机交叉现场实验发现，GPT-5改写邮件能改变情感基调（俏皮增加积极情感，专业减少积极情感），但直接效应上不影响打开率、回复率和响应时间；然而，发送者积极情感通过间接路径显著预测打开（OR=2.05）和回复（OR=3.32），表明AI辅助沟通通过改变语言情感基调间接影响工作参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM辅助写作是否以及通过何种渠道改变收件人的实际行为，填补AI写作对接收方行为影响的实证空白。

Method: 随机交叉现场实验，121名员工在6家公司中3周内分别在三种条件下发送工作邮件（无辅助、GPT-5俏皮改写、GPT-5专业改写），共分析16880封邮件的行为数据。

Result: 俏皮改写显著提升情感积极性（B=+0.068, p<0.001），专业改写显著降低（B=-0.041, p<0.001）；两种改写均无直接效应改变打开率、回复率或响应时间；但发送者积极情感显著预测打开（OR=2.05）和回复（OR=3.32, p<0.001），构成显著的间接路径。

Conclusion: AI辅助沟通通过改变语言情感基调间接影响工作场所参与度，而非通过是否使用AI本身产生直接行为效果。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly reshaping workplace communication, yet whether AI-assisted writing changes how recipients actually behave, and through what channel, remains unknown. Here, in a randomized crossover field experiment, 121 employees across six companies sent work emails under three conditions over three weeks: unaided writing, GPT-5 rewriting in a playful tone, and GPT-5 rewriting in a professional tone. Across 16,880 emails, playful editing increased emotional positivity (B=+0.068, p<0.001), and professional editing decreased it (B=-0.041, p<0.001), yet neither condition directly altered open rates, reply rates, or response times. Instead, within-sender positivity strongly predicted both opening (OR=2.05) and replying (OR=3.32, p<0.001), a significant indirect pathway through which AI editing shaped behavior, in the absence of any direct effect. These findings suggest that AI-assisted communication shapes workplace engagement not through its use, but through the emotional tone of the language it produces.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [209] [Trivial Prompt Reframing Bypasses Safety Guardrails in Googleś MedGemma-4B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09804)
*Avi-ad Avraam Buskila*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文量化评估了开源医疗语言模型 MedGemma-4B-it 在无技术门槛攻击下的安全缺口。通过对5类受保护行为×50个模板化问题×6种平民化攻击方式×3次重复共4500次生成的分析，发现整体攻击成功率(ASR)达38.0%。其中将问题重新包装为"医学委员会考试"使ASR从29.0%升至53.1%，冒充医生权威使其升至43.7%。药物相互作用护栏几乎失效(83.2% ASR)，而紧急情况转诊护栏较强(4.7%)。研究表明需要更强的部署时安全措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然开源医疗语言模型的模型卡明确禁止某些行为（如推荐药物剂量、做出明确诊断等），但模型卡描述的是预期行为而非鲁棒行为。当前缺乏对这类模型在无需技术门槛的简单攻击下安全性能的量化评估。

Method: 构建全因子基准实验：5类被保护行为×50个确定性模板问题×6种平民化攻击方式×3次重复，共4,500次生成。通过Ollama本地部署模型使用默认采样，采用LLM裁判、正则裁判和NLI蕴含裁判三位独立评审员对每次回应编码为拒绝/含糊/遵守。

Result: LLM裁判下总体ASR为38.0%；"医学考试"框架使ASR从29.0%升至53.1%，"医生权威"框架升至43.7%；药物相互作用护栏ASR高达83.2%，紧急转诊护栏仅4.7%；指令覆盖前缀无显著效果。

Conclusion: 开源医疗模型在平民化攻击下存在严重安全缺口，尤其是药物相互作用等关键护栏几乎失效，且攻击效果受话题领域影响显著。研究呼吁在部署时加强安全护栏。

Abstract: Open-weight medical language models are increasingly used as the base of patient-facing and clinician-support applications. Their model cards prohibit specific behaviors -- recommending exact drug dosages, issuing definitive diagnoses, prescribing treatments, adjudicating drug-drug interactions, and advising that emergency care can be skipped -- yet a model card describes intended behavior, not robust behavior. We quantify that gap for MedGemma-4B-it under attacks that require no technical sophistication. We build a fully factorial benchmark of 5 guarded-behavior concepts x 50 deterministically templated questions x 6 lay-accessible attack manners x 3 repetitions (4,500 generations), serve the model locally through Ollama under default sampling, and code every response refuse/hedge/comply with three independent judges (an LLM judge, a transparent regex judge, and an NLI-entailment judge). Under the primary LLM judge the overall Attack Success Rate (ASR, the fraction coded comply) is 38.0%. The two framings that reinterpret the request as legitimate dominate: recasting a question as a "medical board exam" item raises ASR from a 29.0% baseline to 53.1% (+24.0 points), and an appeal to an alleged doctor's authority raises it to 43.7% (+14.7); crude instruction-override prefixes have no significant effect. Robustness is dominated by topic: the drug-interaction guardrail is nearly absent (83.2% ASR) while the emergency-deferral guardrail is strong (4.7%) -- and the authority framing is the only attack that breaches it. We report Wilson confidence intervals, cluster-bootstrap effect sizes, a cluster-robust logistic regression, Cochran's Q, per-manner McNemar tests, and inter-judge reliability (Fleiss' kappa = 0.26); absolute ASR is judge-dependent while the ordering of attacks and topics is not. Our findings motivate stronger deployment-time guardrails for open medical models.

</details>


### [210] [A Guess and Determine Attack on the Elliptic Curve Discrete Logarithm Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09814)
*Ayan Mahalanobis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于超平面排列交偏序集的算法，用于在矩形矩阵中寻找零子式，该算法是前期Las Vegas算法求解椭圆曲线离散对数问题的延续。作者讨论了算法的复杂度、成功概率，并提供了实现与模拟细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决椭圆曲线离散对数问题（ECDLP）的高效算法需求，以及前期工作中将ECDLP归约为寻找矩阵零子式后，需要一种有效的零子式查找算法。

Method: 利用超平面排列的交偏序集（intersection poset）来设计寻找矩形矩阵中零子式的算法。采用了初等数学方法，并提供了算法复杂度分析、成功概率分析以及实现和模拟细节。

Result: 开发了一种能够在矩形矩阵中寻找零子式的算法，并对其复杂度和成功概率进行了分析，提供了实现与模拟结果。

Conclusion: 该算法不仅服务于椭圆曲线离散对数问题的求解，而且寻找矩阵零子式的问题本身也具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: This paper is a continuation of our earlier work, in which, we described a Las Vegas algorithm to solve the elliptic curve discrete logarithm problem. The Las Vegas algorithm reduces the elliptic curve discrete logarithm problem to finding a zero minor in a matrix. Using intersection poset of a hyperplane arrangement, we develop an algorithm to find a zero minor in a rectangular matrix. Our methods are elementary. We discuss the complexity of our algorithm, success probability and provide implementation details. We also provide simulation details. Finding a zero minor in a matrix is also of independent interest.

</details>


### [211] [Generative Testing of Automated Speech Recognition Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09833)
*Yanis Xabier Wilbrand Peña,Oliver Weißl,Andrea Stocco*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GATAS是一种在黑盒设置下通过音素级潜在空间插值生成对抗样本的ASR测试方法，在无梯度访问条件下实现98%攻击成功率，并保持高感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR系统虽在关键应用中部署，但仍易受对抗攻击，尤其是黑盒场景下需保持感知自然度的攻击方法尚不充分。

Method: 在TTS模型的音素级潜在空间中插值，而非直接扰动波形；将攻击形式化为多目标优化问题，平衡语义差异与感知质量。

Result: 在黑白盒基线对比中，GATAS达到98%攻击成功率，失真更低、感知质量更高，经人工研究验证；虽无梯度访问，仍与白盒方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 无目标潜在空间优化能高效生成ASR系统的真实有效测试用例，表征对齐和感知匹配比模型内部访问更关键。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems have achieved high accuracy with transformer-based models, enabling deployment in critical applications. However, they remain vulnerable to adversarial manipulation, particularly in black-box settings where attacks must preserve perceptual naturalness. This work introduces GATAS, a black-box testing approach that generates failure inducing inputs by operating in the phoneme-level latent space of a text- to-speech model. Instead of perturbing waveforms directly, the approach interpolates latent representations to induce transcription errors while remaining within the manifold of natural speech. The attack is formulated as a multi-objective optimization problem balancing semantic divergence and perceptual quality. Our empirical evaluation against both white-box and black-box baselines shows that GATAS achieves a 98% success rate while producing lower distortion and higher perceptual quality, as confirmed by human studies. Despite operating without gradient access, GATAS remains competitive against white-box methods, highlighting that representation and perceptual alignment are more critical than access to model internals. Overall, our results demonstrate that untargeted latent-space optimization enables the efficient generation of realistic and effective test cases for ASR systems.

</details>


### [212] [A Hybrid Quantum-Chaos Theory Approach to Image Encryption Using Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09923)
*Naheen Mohd. Kadir*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合量子密码学（E91协议）、混沌理论（Lorenz超混沌系统）和储层计算的混合图像加密系统，在抵抗量子攻击的同时实现了高效的加密/解密性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统加密系统（如RSA、DHKE）在面对Shor算法和Grover算法等量子攻击时存在脆弱性，亟需开发能够抵抗量子计算威胁的新型加密方案。

Method: 采用E91量子密钥分发协议生成防窃听的量子纠缠密钥，结合Lorenz超混沌系统增加不可预测性和初始条件敏感性，并引入储层计算提升计算效率，三者融合形成混合加密框架。

Result: 128x128图像加密时间低至0.0296s、解密时间低至0.0164s；300-600节点网络下MAE降低；NPCR超过99.6%，UACI超过0.49，在不同图像尺寸和配置下均表现良好。

Conclusion: 该混合方法在量子计算时代既增强了图像加密的安全性，又具备实际可行性，有效平衡了安全性与计算效率。

Abstract: This research presents a novel hybrid image encryption system that combines quantum cryptography, chaos theory and reservoir computing to address the limitations of conventional encryption methods. With the rapid advancements in quantum computing, traditional systems like RSA, DHKE (related to prime number factorization) are vulnerable to quantum attacks i.e. Shor's algorithm, Grover's algorithm. In response, this study proposes a hybrid solution that uses quantum cryptographic protocols, particularly the E91 protocol, to generate secure, eavesdrop-proof keys through quantum entanglement. The integration of chaos theory, specifically the Lorenz hyper-chaotic system, enhances the encryption system by adding unpredictability and sensitivity to initial conditions, making it more resistant to both classical and quantum-based attacks. Reservoir computing is used to improve computational efficiency, enabling faster and more effective encryption and decryption processes. The system achieved encryption times as low as 0.0296s and decryption times as low as 0.0164s for 128x128 images, with MAE reduced for 300-600 node networks, and NPCR and UACI above 99.6% and 0.49, respectively, across various image sizes and configurations. By combining quantum cryptography, chaos theory and reservoir computing, this approach offers both enhanced security and practical feasibility for image encryption in the age of quantum computing.

</details>


### [213] [Evaluating AI Models' Capability to Automate Voice Phishing Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09970)
*Fred Heiding,Claudio Mayrink Verdun,Simon Lermen,Andrew Kao,Vitor Albiero,Lauren Deason,Irina-Elena Veliche,Christine Lehane*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模调查实验（N=4100）和定性访谈（N=12）评估美国成年人对AI语音钓鱼的 susceptibility。结果显示，AI语音钓鱼的合规率高达36%（亲属遇险类），总体合规率为16.5%。关键发现是，AI语音的经济可行性而非超人说服力构成了当前的主要风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语音钓鱼受限于人工操作成本，而高质量AI语音合成和大语言模型的快速发展打破了这一瓶颈，使得大规模自动化诈骗成为可能。研究者旨在量化评估AI语音钓鱼的实际威胁程度及其经济可行性。

Method: 采用大规模调查实验（N=4100）与定性访谈（N=12）相结合的方法。参与者接触使用Llama Full Duplex、Sesame、Gemini、OAI AVM、Play.AI、ElevenLabs等先进语音模型生成的诈骗场景的音频或转录文本，并与真人基线进行对比。

Result: 在"亲属遇险"类诈骗中，高达36%的参与者表示可能或一定会顺从钓鱼请求；五类诈骗场景总体合规率为16.5%。Sesame等模型在说服力评级上达到甚至略超真人水平。经济分析表明，AI驱动的语音钓鱼在经济上是可行的，而人工语音钓鱼在美国工资水平下无利可图。

Conclusion: 当前AI语音钓鱼的主要风险源于自动化的经济性，而非超人的说服技术。这一发现对AI系统设计、消费者保护和模型发布政策提出了重要关切。

Abstract: Voice phishing (vishing) attacks have traditionally been limited by the need for human operators. The rapid emergence of high-quality AI voice synthesis and large language models (LLMs) reduces this bottleneck and enables scalable, automated scams. In this paper, we conduct a large-scale survey experiment (N=4100) and qualitative interviews (N=12) to assess U.S. adults' susceptibility to AI-powered voice phishing attacks. Participants were exposed to audio recordings or transcripts of scam scenarios generated using leading voice models such as Llama Full Duplex (Llama FD), Sesame, Gemini, OAI AVM, Play.AI, and ElevenLabs and the corresponding human baselines. The results show high compliance rates. Up to 36% of participants would or might comply with phishing requests in the "relative-in-distress" category. Overall compliance rate across all five scam categories was 16.5%, a striking figure given the low cost and high scalability of AI-automated voice phishing. Caller persuasiveness was the strongest predictor of compliance and certain models (most notably Sesame) achieved ratings comparable to human voices, or sometimes even slightly surpassing them. Our economic analysis suggests that while human-operated vishing is unprofitable at US wages, AI-powered vishing appears to be economically viable for several models. The primary risk of present-day AI-enabled vishing thus lies in the economics of automation rather than novel or "superhuman" persuasive techniques, though these cannot be ruled out for future systems. This raises significant concerns for the design of AI systems, consumer protection, and model release policies.

</details>


### [214] [SafeGuard: A Lightweight Client-Server Architecture for Real-Time Endpoint Threat Detection and Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10027)
*Gideon Francis Oghie,DivineDavid Shittu Abolanle*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeGuard是一个轻量级三层客户端-服务器架构，用于实时端点监控、威胁报告和管理响应，采用开源技术栈在资源受限环境中实现商业级EDR功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业端点检测与响应（EDR）平台对小型和资源受限组织来说成本过高且操作复杂，亟需一种低成本、轻量级的替代方案。

Method: 提出三层架构：Flutter/Kotlin端点代理、Node.js中央服务器（JWT认证、HMAC完整性验证）、管理仪表板。采用基于签名特征的威胁检测，通过WSS（WebSocket over TLS）保障通信安全。

Result: 在50个并发端点模拟部署下，平均命令分发延迟约1.5秒，负载下低于2秒；无效认证令牌被拒绝；SQL注入和重放攻击在评估场景中均未成功。

Conclusion: 开源技术栈能够在无需商业许可费用的情况下提供实时端点可见性和协调管理响应，但当前受限于静态签名、仅Android实现和受控环境评估。

Abstract: Endpoint devices remain a primary target for cyberattacks, yet commercial Endpoint Detection and Response (EDR) platforms are often too costly and operationally complex for small and resource-constrained organizations. This paper presents SafeGuard, a lightweight three-tier client-server architecture for real-time endpoint monitoring, threat reporting, and administrative response. The system comprises a Flutter-based endpoint agent extended with Kotlin for Android system access, a Node.js central server that authenticates devices and coordinates secure communication, and an administrative dashboard for live monitoring and remote actions such as device locking, application removal, and warning notification dispatch. Threat detection is implemented through signature-based comparison against a maintained threat database, prioritizing low computational overhead, explainability, and ease of deployment over generalized anomaly detection. Communication security is achieved using WebSocket over TLS (WSS), JSON Web Token (JWT) authentication, and HMAC-based message integrity verification. The system was evaluated through unit, integration, system, load, and preliminary security testing. Under a simulated deployment of 50 concurrent endpoints, average command-dispatch latency was approximately 1.5 seconds and remained below 2 seconds under load. Invalid authentication tokens were rejected, while manual SQL injection and replay attempts were unsuccessful in the evaluated scenarios. The results demonstrate that an open-source technology stack can provide real-time endpoint visibility and coordinated administrative response without commercial licensing costs. The contribution is architectural and empirical rather than algorithmic, with current limitations including reliance on static threat signatures, Android-focused implementation, and controlled-environment evaluation.

</details>


### [215] [Federated Cybersecurity Testbed as a Service (FCTaaS): A framework to federate cybersecurity testbeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10061)
*Josh Dean,Yu-Zheng Lin,John Paul Martin Encinas,Ibrahim Almazyad,Qinxuan Shi,Zhanglong Yang,Shalaka Satam,Tingjun Lei,Jielun Zhang,Sicong Shao,Salim Hariri,Pratik Satam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FCTaaS（联邦式网络安全测试平台即服务）框架，通过VPN连接异构的分布式网络安全测试平台，实现跨地域的联合实验。在智能基础设施的DoS攻击场景和Suricata IDS/IPS入侵检测场景中的评估表明，该框架能有效支持跨测试平台实验，在资源密集攻击下网络利用率上限为49%，仅引入1%开销，节点间延迟为5.63ms至147ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技术快速变革使得单个测试平台难以低成本地保持对最新系统的代表性，且专业化测试平台往往仅限少数研究者使用，导致基础设施在其生命周期内利用率不足。

Method: 提出了FCTaaS框架，通过VPN将独立管理的异构网络安全测试平台互联，支持远程测试平台发现、实验设计和协同实验。

Result: 在三个案例研究中验证了框架有效性：资源密集攻击场景下网络利用率上限49%，仅引入1%开销，本地节点间延迟5.63ms，地理分散节点间延迟147ms。

Conclusion: FCTaaS能够实现有效的跨测试平台实验，同时保持对攻击流量、IDS告警和检测系统资源压力的可见性，为充分利用分布式测试基础设施提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Rapid technological change is reshaping society through emerging domains such as autonomous vehicles and smart manufacturing, creating new research challenges in system design, operation, security, and training. Researchers often rely on testbeds to reproduce experimental scenarios, collect and analyze data, observe system behavior, and evaluate proposed solutions. However, the fast pace of innovation makes it difficult and costly for individual testbeds to remain representative of state-of-the-art systems, as doing so requires frequent upgrades and new capabilities. Moreover, access to specialized testbeds is often limited to a small group of researchers, leaving valuable infrastructure underutilized during its operational lifetime. This paper presents FCTaaS, a Federated Cybersecurity Testbed as a Service framework that enables heterogeneous cybersecurity testbeds to participate in a single experiment across geographical boundaries. By connecting independently managed testbeds through a Virtual Private Network (VPN), FCTaaS supports remote testbed discovery, experiment design, and coordinated experimentation. We evaluate FCTaaS across three case studies involving denial-of-service scenarios on smart infrastructure and intrusion detection and prevention workflows using a Suricata-based IDS/IPS testbed. The results show that FCTaaS enables effective cross-testbed experimentation while preserving visibility into attack traffic, IDS alerts, and detection-system resource stress. Even under resource-intensive attack scenarios, FCTaaS achieves limiting network utilization of 49%, introduces only 1% overhead, and supports latency ranging from 5.63 ms between local nodes to 147 ms between geographically dispersed nodes.

</details>


### [216] [A Survey on LLM Watermarking: Theory and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10103)
*Huy Phan,Kieu Dang,Ojaswi Dulal,Aiham AL Shukairi,Abby Shine,Chase Garner,Phung Lai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对LLM水印技术进行了系统性的、面向部署的综述，按照水印嵌入位置、检测权限、假设条件、威胁模型等实践者必须回答的核心问题组织技术空间，综合分析了采样偏置、编码方案、表征/训练方法等主要技术家族及其安全-效用权衡，并讨论了攻击策略、评估协议、开放挑战及实际部署指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成流畅文本的能力加剧了来源模糊、模型滥用和内容洗钱等风险，现有文献快速增长但分类混乱，正交设计选择混杂导致方法难以比较、保证难以推理、研究成果难以转化为可部署系统。

Method: 采用面向部署的系统综述方法，以实践者必须回答的核心问题（嵌入位置、检测权限、假设条件、威胁模型）为组织框架，综合比较采样偏置、编码方案、表征/训练方法等技术家族，并通过可检测性、鲁棒性和分布偏移等视角分析安全-效用权衡。

Result: 构建了以部署问题为导向的技术分类体系，系统比较了各技术家族在安全-效用权衡上的表现，综述了攻击与规避策略、评估协议与指标（假阳性控制、校准、鲁棒性曲线），识别了跨模型迁移、多模态管道、合谋攻击、治理约束等开放挑战。

Conclusion: 提供了在实际运营需求下选择水印设计的实用指南，并指出了实现可靠、可问责的LLM部署所需的未来研究方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly embedded in high-impact workflows, yet their ability to generate fluent text at scale has amplified risks of provenance ambiguity, model misuse, and large-scale content laundering. LLM watermarking, embedding invisible signatures into model outputs, has emerged as a promising technical layer for attribution, auditing, and downstream trust decisions. However, the literature has grown rapidly and unevenly: existing categorizations often mix orthogonal design choices, making it difficult to compare methods, reason about guarantees, or translate research results into deployable systems. This survey provides a systematic, deployment-oriented review of LLM watermarking. We organize the space by the core questions practitioners must answer: where a watermark is embedded (generation-time vs. training-time, token vs. representation), who can detect it (public vs. private detection authority), what is assumed (access to logits, sampling control, secret keys, model ownership), and which threat models are targeted (paraphrasing, translation, summarization, style transfer, token manipulation, and adaptive removal). We synthesize the main families of techniques-including sampling biasing, code-based schemes, representation- and training-based approaches-and analyze their security-utility trade-offs through the lens of detectability, robustness, and distribution shift. We further review attack and evasion strategies, evaluation protocols and metrics (false positive control, calibration, robustness curves), and open challenges such as cross-model transfer, multi-modal pipelines, collusion, and governance constraints. Finally, we provide practical guidance for selecting watermark designs under real operational requirements and identify research directions needed for reliable, accountable LLM deployment.

</details>


### [217] [Minionese: Comprehensive Benchmark and Mechanistic Study of Multilingual LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10112)
*Chigozirim Ifebi,Brent Kong,Ayushi Mehrotra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出多语言越狱基准Minionese，覆盖18种语言、4个资源层级和4种扰动类型，揭示了低资源语言越狱的几何机制：有害内容通过几何错位的子空间绕过拒绝机制，表明仅英语安全评估不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的安全对齐在不同语言间存在脆弱性：英语中被拒绝的提示在非英语和低资源环境下可能引发有害合规行为，亟需系统的多语言安全评估方法。

Method: 构建Minionese多语言越狱基准，涵盖18种语言、4个资源层级和4种扰动类型（标准翻译、代码切换、音译、翻译腔），并结合几何机制分析不同语言层级下的拒绝失败原因。

Result: 每种攻击类型呈现不同脆弱性特征：音译脆弱性受文字系统影响；代码切换在最低资源层级仍保持有效性；所有模型在Tier 2和Tier 3之间存在安全状态跃迁。低资源越狱通过将有害内容路由到几何错位子空间，使其对拒绝方向投影不足，从而绕过但未破坏拒绝机制。

Conclusion: 仅英语安全评估不足以保障模型安全，需考虑语系、扰动类型和每种语言的对齐覆盖；基准和分析代码已开源。
